Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the thermodynamics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 747 and the convergence rate is O(126/n^4).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the thermodynamics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^747 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 126-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
